### User

_source line 212 · 2026-05-18T08:56:44.643Z_

This session is being continued from a previous conversation that ran out of context. The summary below covers the earlier portion of the conversation.

Summary:
1. Primary Request and Intent:
   The user wants a local SCS vs Simple Baselines comparison analysis. The goal is to compare SCS (embedding cosine similarity) with three simple baseline metrics for predicting pairwise type confusion, in response to a reviewer request on their paper. The three baselines are: (1) Normalized Levenshtein Similarity, (2) WordNet Wu-Palmer Similarity, and (3) Same-parent Indicator. The analysis targets FewRel (80 relations, 3160 pairs) and Few-NERD (63 types, 1953 pairs). Output should be a `results.json` in a specific format plus a summary table, saved to `artifacts/scs_baseline_comparison/`.

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - SCS (Schema Cosine Similarity): embedding-based cosine similarity between type/relation embeddings
   - Pairwise type confusion: how often an LLM confuses one type for another in NER/RE tasks
   - Spearman rank correlation between similarity metrics and confusion frequency
   - FewRel: 80-relation RE dataset; SCS computed from MiniLM embeddings on remote
   - Few-NERD: 66-type NER dataset (63 with gold entities); SCS from Qwen3 mean-pool embeddings
   - Normalized Levenshtein distance: 1 - edit_distance/max(len)
   - WordNet Wu-Palmer similarity: semantic similarity via lowest common subsumer depth
   - Same-parent indicator: whether two types share a direct WordNet hypernym (or for Few-NERD, share prefix category)

}

Pre-computed results from remote\nFEWREL_SCS = {\"rho\": 0.218238, \"p\": 2.219981737602145e-35, \"n_pairs\": 3160}\nFEWREL_LEV = {\"rho\": 0.016186, \"p\": 0.3630431290498525, \"n_pairs\": 3160}\nFEWNERD_SCS = {\"rho\": 0.16616, \"p\": 1.4706429598501858e-13, \"n_pairs\": 1953}\nFEWNERD_LEV = {\"rho\": 0.152227, \"p\": 1.3563843339646998e-11, \"n_pairs\": 1953}\n\nOUTPUT_DIR = Path(__file__).parent\n\n\ndef get_pairs(types):\n    return list(itertools.combinations(sorted(types), 2))\n\n\ndef normalized_levenshtein(s1, s2):\n    m, n = len(s1), len(s2)\n    if m == 0 and n == 0:\n        return 1.0\n    dp = list(range(n + 1))\n    for i in range(1, m + 1):\n        prev = dp[0]\n        dp[0] = i\n        for j in range(1, n + 1):\n            temp = dp[j]\n            if s1[i-1] == s2[j-1]:\n                dp[j] = prev\n            else:\n                dp[j] = 1 + min(prev, dp[j], dp[j-1])\n            prev = temp\n    return 1.0 - dp[n] / max(m, n)\n\n\ndef wordnet_wup_similarity(name1, name2):\n    from nltk.corpus import wordnet as wn\n\n    def get_synset(name):\n        name = name.lower().strip()\n        # For Few-NERD hierarchical names, take part after hyphen\n        if \"-\" in name:\n            name = name.split(\"-\", 1)[1]\n        # For multi-word names with slashes, take first part\n        if \"/\" in name:\n            name = name.split(\"/\")[0]\n        # Clean up\n        name = name.replace(\" \", \"_\")\n\n        # Try direct lookup\n        synsets = wn.synsets(name, pos=wn.NOUN)\n        if synsets:\n            return synsets[0]\n\n        # Try individual words (last word first as head noun)\n        words = name.replace(\"_\", \" \").split()\n        for w in reversed(words):\n            synsets = wn.synsets(w, pos=wn.NOUN)\n            if synsets:\n                return synsets[0]\n\n        # Try without pos restriction\n        synsets = wn.synsets(name)\n        if synsets:\n            return synsets[0]\n        for w in reversed(words):\n            synsets = wn.synsets(w)\n            if synsets:\n                return synsets[0]\n\n        return None\n\n    s1 = get_synset(name1)\n    s2 = get_synset(name2)\n\n    if s1 is None or s2 is None:\n        return None\n    if s1 == s2:\n        return 1.0\n\n    sim = s1.wup_similarity(s2)\n    return sim\n\n\ndef same_parent_indicator(name1, name2, dataset):\n    if dataset == \"fewnerd\":\n        # Same prefix category\n        cat1 = name1.split(\"-\", 1)[0] if \"-\" in name1 else name1\n        cat2 = name2.split(\"-\", 1)[0] if \"-\" in name2 else name2\n        return 1 if cat1 == cat2 else 0\n    else:\n        # FewRel: check if they share a direct WordNet hypernym\n        from nltk.corpus import wordnet as wn\n\n        def get_hypernyms(name):\n            name = name.lower().strip().replace(\" \", \"_\")\n            synsets = wn.synsets(name, pos=wn.NOUN)\n            if not synsets:\n                words = name.replace(\"_\", \" \").split()\n                for w in reversed(words):\n                    synsets = wn.synsets(w, pos=wn.NOUN)\n                    if synsets:\n                        break\n            if not synsets:\n                synsets = wn.synsets(name)\n                if not synsets:\n                    words = name.replace(\"_\", \" \").split()\n                    for w in reversed(words):\n                        synsets = wn.synsets(w)\n                        if synsets:\n                            break\n            if not synsets:\n                return set()\n            return set(h for s in synsets[:2] for h in s.hypernyms())\n\n        h1 = get_hypernyms(name1)\n        h2 = get_hypernyms(name2)\n        return 1 if h1 & h2 else 0\n\n\ndef compute_metrics(types, conf_array, dataset_name):\n    pairs = get_pairs(types)\n    n_pairs = len(pairs)\n    assert len(conf_array) == n_pairs, f\"Expected {n_pairs} pairs, got {len(conf_array)}\"\n\n    conf = np.array(conf_array, dtype=float)\n\n    wup_vals = []\n    parent_vals = []\n    wup_missing = 0\n\n    for i, (t1, t2) in enumerate(pairs):\n        wup = wordnet_wup_similarity(t1, t2)\n        if wup is None:\n            wup_vals.append(np.nan)\n            wup_missing += 1\n        else:\n            wup_vals.append(wup)\n\n        parent_vals.append(same_parent_indicator(t1, t2, dataset_name))\n\n    wup_arr = np.array(wup_vals)\n    parent_arr = np.array(parent_vals, dtype=float)\n\n    # WuP correlation (exclude NaN pairs)\n    valid_wup = ~np.isnan(wup_arr)\n    n_valid_wup = valid_wup.sum()\n    if n_valid_wup > 10:\n        wup_rho, wup_p = stats.spearmanr(wup_arr[valid_wup], conf[valid_wup])\n    else:\n        wup_rho, wup_p = float(\"nan\"), float(\"nan\")\n\n    # Same-parent correlation\n    parent_rho, parent_p = stats.spearmanr(parent_arr, conf)\n\n    print(f\"\\n--- {dataset_name} ---\")\n    print(f\"  Total pairs: {n_pairs}\")\n    print(f\"  WuP: valid={n_valid_wup}, missing={wup_missing}, rho={wup_rho:.6f}, p={wup_p:.2e}\")\n    print(f\"  Same-parent: rho={parent_rho:.6f}, p={parent_p:.2e}\")\n    print(f\"  Same-parent=1 count: {int(parent_arr.sum())}\")\n\n    return {\n        \"wup\": {\"rho\": float(wup_rho), \"p\": float(wup_p), \"n_pairs\": int(n_valid_wup), \"n_missing\": wup_missing},\n        \"same_parent\": {\"rho\": float(parent_rho), \"p\": float(parent_p), \"n_pairs\": n_pairs},\n    }\n\n\ndef main():\n    import nltk\n    nltk.download(\"wordnet\", quiet=True)\n    nltk.download(\"omw-1.4\", quiet=True)\n\n    print(\"Computing WordNet WuP and same-parent for FewRel...\")\n    fewrel_results = compute_metrics(FEWREL_TYPES, FEWREL_CONF, \"fewrel\")\n\n    print(\"\\nComputing WordNet WuP and same-parent for Few-NERD...\")\n    fewnerd_results = compute_metrics(FEWNERD_TYPES, FEWNERD_CONF, \"fewnerd\")\n\n    results = {\n        \"fewrel\": {\n            \"n_types\": len(FEWREL_TYPES),\n            \"n_pairs\": FEWREL_SCS[\"n_pairs\"],\n            \"metrics\": {\n                \"scs\": {\"spearman_rho\": FEWREL_SCS[\"rho\"], \"p_value\": FEWREL_SCS[\"p\"]},\n                \"levenshtein\": {\"spearman_rho\": FEWREL_LEV[\"rho\"], \"p_value\": FEWREL_LEV[\"p\"]},\n                \"wordnet_wup\": {\"spearman_rho\": fewrel_results[\"wup\"][\"rho\"], \"p_value\": fewrel_results[\"wup\"][\"p\"], \"n_valid_pairs\": fewrel_results[\"wup\"][\"n_pairs\"], \"n_missing\": fewrel_results[\"wup\"][\"n_missing\"]},\n                \"same_parent\": {\"spearman_rho\": fewrel_results[\"same_parent\"][\"rho\"], \"p_value\": fewrel_results[\"same_parent\"][\"p\"]},\n            },\n        },\n        \"fewnerd\": {\n            \"n_types\": len(FEWNERD_TYPES),\n            \"n_pairs\": FEWNERD_SCS[\"n_pairs\"],\n            \"metrics\": {\n                \"scs\": {\"spearman_rho\": FEWNERD_SCS[\"rho\"], \"p_value\": FEWNERD_SCS[\"p\"]},\n                \"levenshtein\": {\"spearman_rho\": FEWNERD_LEV[\"rho\"], \"p_value\": FEWNERD_LEV[\"p\"]},\n                \"wordnet_wup\": {\"spearman_rho\": fewnerd_results[\"wup\"][\"rho\"], \"p_value\": fewnerd_results[\"wup\"][\"p\"], \"n_valid_pairs\": fewnerd_results[\"wup\"][\"n_pairs\"], \"n_missing\": fewnerd_results[\"wup\"][\"n_missing\"]},\n                \"same_parent\": {\"spearman_rho\": fewnerd_results[\"same_parent\"][\"rho\"], \"p_value\": fewnerd_results[\"same_parent\"][\"p\"]},\n            },\n        },\n    }\n\n    output_path = OUTPUT_DIR / \"results.json\"\n    with open(output_path, \"w\") as f:\n        json.dump(results, f, indent=2)\n    print(f\"\\nSaved results to {output_path}\")\n\n    # Summary table\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 85)\n    print(f\"{'Dataset':<12} {'Metric':<20} {'Spearman ρ':>12} {'p-value':>14} {'Sig?':>6}\")\n    print(\"-\" * 85)\n    for ds in [\"fewrel\", \"fewnerd\"]:\n        for metric in [\"scs\", \"levenshtein\", \"wordnet_wup\", \"same_parent\"]:\n            m = results[ds][\"metrics\"][metric]\n            rho = m[\"spearman_rho\"]\n            p = m[\"p_value\"]\n            sig = \"***\" if p < 0.001 else \"**\" if p < 0.01 else \"*\" if p < 0.05 else \"ns\"\n            print(f\"{ds:<12} {metric:<20} {rho:>12.4f} {p:>14.2e} {sig:>6}\")\n        print(\"-\" * 85)\n    print(\"=\" * 85)\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n"

Pre-computed results from remote\nFEWREL_SCS = {\"rho\": 0.218238, \"p\": 2.219981737602145e-35, \"n_pairs\": 3160}\nFEWREL_LEV = {\"rho\": 0.016186, \"p\": 0.3630431290498525, \"n_pairs\": 3160}\nFEWNERD_SCS = {\"rho\": 0.16616, \"p\": 1.4706429598501858e-13, \"n_pairs\": 1953}\nFEWNERD_LEV = {\"rho\": 0.152227, \"p\": 1.3563843339646998e-11, \"n_pairs\": 1953}\n\nOUTPUT_DIR = Path(__file__).parent\n\n\ndef get_pairs(types):\n    return list(itertools.combinations(sorted(types), 2))\n\n\ndef normalized_levenshtein(s1, s2):\n    m, n = len(s1), len(s2)\n    if m == 0 and n == 0:\n        return 1.0\n    dp = list(range(n + 1))\n    for i in range(1, m + 1):\n        prev = dp[0]\n        dp[0] = i\n        for j in range(1, n + 1):\n            temp = dp[j]\n            if s1[i-1] == s2[j-1]:\n                dp[j] = prev\n            else:\n                dp[j] = 1 + min(prev, dp[j], dp[j-1])\n            prev = temp\n    return 1.0 - dp[n] / max(m, n)\n\n\ndef wordnet_wup_similarity(name1, name2):\n    from nltk.corpus import wordnet as wn\n\n    def get_synset(name):\n        name = name.lower().strip()\n        # For Few-NERD hierarchical names, take part after hyphen\n        if \"-\" in name:\n            name = name.split(\"-\", 1)[1]\n        # For multi-word names with slashes, take first part\n        if \"/\" in name:\n            name = name.split(\"/\")[0]\n        # Clean up\n        name = name.replace(\" \", \"_\")\n\n        # Try direct lookup\n        synsets = wn.synsets(name, pos=wn.NOUN)\n        if synsets:\n            return synsets[0]\n\n        # Try individual words (last word first as head noun)\n        words = name.replace(\"_\", \" \").split()\n        for w in reversed(words):\n            synsets = wn.synsets(w, pos=wn.NOUN)\n            if synsets:\n                return synsets[0]\n\n        # Try without pos restriction\n        synsets = wn.synsets(name)\n        if synsets:\n            return synsets[0]\n        for w in reversed(words):\n            synsets = wn.synsets(w)\n            if synsets:\n                return synsets[0]\n\n        return None\n\n    s1 = get_synset(name1)\n    s2 = get_synset(name2)\n\n    if s1 is None or s2 is None:\n        return None\n    if s1 == s2:\n        return 1.0\n\n    sim = s1.wup_similarity(s2)\n    return sim\n\n\ndef same_parent_indicator(name1, name2, dataset):\n    if dataset == \"fewnerd\":\n        # Same prefix category\n        cat1 = name1.split(\"-\", 1)[0] if \"-\" in name1 else name1\n        cat2 = name2.split(\"-\", 1)[0] if \"-\" in name2 else name2\n        return 1 if cat1 == cat2 else 0\n    else:\n        # FewRel: check if they share a direct WordNet hypernym\n        from nltk.corpus import wordnet as wn\n\n        def get_hypernyms(name):\n            name = name.lower().strip().replace(\" \", \"_\")\n            synsets = wn.synsets(name, pos=wn.NOUN)\n            if not synsets:\n                words = name.replace(\"_\", \" \").split()\n                for w in reversed(words):\n                    synsets = wn.synsets(w, pos=wn.NOUN)\n                    if synsets:\n                        break\n            if not synsets:\n                synsets = wn.synsets(name)\n                if not synsets:\n                    words = name.replace(\"_\", \" \").split()\n                    for w in reversed(words):\n                        synsets = wn.synsets(w)\n                        if synsets:\n                            break\n            if not synsets:\n                return set()\n            return set(h for s in synsets[:2] for h in s.hypernyms())\n\n        h1 = get_hypernyms(name1)\n        h2 = get_hypernyms(name2)\n        return 1 if h1 & h2 else 0\n\n\ndef compute_metrics(types, conf_array, dataset_name):\n    pairs = get_pairs(types)\n    n_pairs = len(pairs)\n    assert len(conf_array) == n_pairs, f\"Expected {n_pairs} pairs, got {len(conf_array)}\"\n\n    conf = np.array(conf_array, dtype=float)\n\n    wup_vals = []\n    parent_vals = []\n    wup_missing = 0\n\n    for i, (t1, t2) in enumerate(pairs):\n        wup = wordnet_wup_similarity(t1, t2)\n        if wup is None:\n            wup_vals.append(np.nan)\n            wup_missing += 1\n        else:\n            wup_vals.append(wup)\n\n        parent_vals.append(same_parent_indicator(t1, t2, dataset_name))\n\n    wup_arr = np.array(wup_vals)\n    parent_arr = np.array(parent_vals, dtype=float)\n\n    # WuP correlation (exclude NaN pairs)\n    valid_wup = ~np.isnan(wup_arr)\n    n_valid_wup = valid_wup.sum()\n    if n_valid_wup > 10:\n        wup_rho, wup_p = stats.spearmanr(wup_arr[valid_wup], conf[valid_wup])\n    else:\n        wup_rho, wup_p = float(\"nan\"), float(\"nan\")\n\n    # Same-parent correlation\n    parent_rho, parent_p = stats.spearmanr(parent_arr, conf)\n\n    print(f\"\\n--- {dataset_name} ---\")\n    print(f\"  Total pairs: {n_pairs}\")\n    print(f\"  WuP: valid={n_valid_wup}, missing={wup_missing}, rho={wup_rho:.6f}, p={wup_p:.2e}\")\n    print(f\"  Same-parent: rho={parent_rho:.6f}, p={parent_p:.2e}\")\n    print(f\"  Same-parent=1 count: {int(parent_arr.sum())}\")\n\n    return {\n        \"wup\": {\"rho\": float(wup_rho), \"p\": float(wup_p), \"n_pairs\": int(n_valid_wup), \"n_missing\": wup_missing},\n        \"same_parent\": {\"rho\": float(parent_rho), \"p\": float(parent_p), \"n_pairs\": n_pairs},\n    }\n\n\ndef main():\n    import nltk\n    nltk.download(\"wordnet\", quiet=True)\n    nltk.download(\"omw-1.4\", quiet=True)\n\n    print(\"Computing WordNet WuP and same-parent for FewRel...\")\n    fewrel_results = compute_metrics(FEWREL_TYPES, FEWREL_CONF, \"fewrel\")\n\n    print(\"\\nComputing WordNet WuP and same-parent for Few-NERD...\")\n    fewnerd_results = compute_metrics(FEWNERD_TYPES, FEWNERD_CONF, \"fewnerd\")\n\n    results = {\n        \"fewrel\": {\n            \"n_types\": len(FEWREL_TYPES),\n            \"n_pairs\": FEWREL_SCS[\"n_pairs\"],\n            \"metrics\": {\n                \"scs\": {\"spearman_rho\": FEWREL_SCS[\"rho\"], \"p_value\": FEWREL_SCS[\"p\"]},\n                \"levenshtein\": {\"spearman_rho\": FEWREL_LEV[\"rho\"], \"p_value\": FEWREL_LEV[\"p\"]},\n                \"wordnet_wup\": {\"spearman_rho\": fewrel_results[\"wup\"][\"rho\"], \"p_value\": fewrel_results[\"wup\"][\"p\"], \"n_valid_pairs\": fewrel_results[\"wup\"][\"n_pairs\"], \"n_missing\": fewrel_results[\"wup\"][\"n_missing\"]},\n                \"same_parent\": {\"spearman_rho\": fewrel_results[\"same_parent\"][\"rho\"], \"p_value\": fewrel_results[\"same_parent\"][\"p\"]},\n            },\n        },\n        \"fewnerd\": {\n            \"n_types\": len(FEWNERD_TYPES),\n            \"n_pairs\": FEWNERD_SCS[\"n_pairs\"],\n            \"metrics\": {\n                \"scs\": {\"spearman_rho\": FEWNERD_SCS[\"rho\"], \"p_value\": FEWNERD_SCS[\"p\"]},\n                \"levenshtein\": {\"spearman_rho\": FEWNERD_LEV[\"rho\"], \"p_value\": FEWNERD_LEV[\"p\"]},\n                \"wordnet_wup\": {\"spearman_rho\": fewnerd_results[\"wup\"][\"rho\"], \"p_value\": fewnerd_results[\"wup\"][\"p\"], \"n_valid_pairs\": fewnerd_results[\"wup\"][\"n_pairs\"], \"n_missing\": fewnerd_results[\"wup\"][\"n_missing\"]},\n                \"same_parent\": {\"spearman_rho\": fewnerd_results[\"same_parent\"][\"rho\"], \"p_value\": fewnerd_results[\"same_parent\"][\"p\"]},\n            },\n        },\n    }\n\n    output_path = OUTPUT_DIR / \"results.json\"\n    with open(output_path, \"w\") as f:\n        json.dump(results, f, indent=2)\n    print(f\"\\nSaved results to {output_path}\")\n\n    # Summary table\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 85)\n    print(f\"{'Dataset':<12} {'Metric':<20} {'Spearman ρ':>12} {'p-value':>14} {'Sig?':>6}\")\n    print(\"-\" * 85)\n    for ds in [\"fewrel\", \"fewnerd\"]:\n        for metric in [\"scs\", \"levenshtein\", \"wordnet_wup\", \"same_parent\"]:\n            m = results[ds][\"metrics\"][metric]\n            rho = m[\"spearman_rho\"]\n            p = m[\"p_value\"]\n            sig = \"***\" if p < 0.001 else \"**\" if p < 0.01 else \"*\" if p < 0.05 else \"ns\"\n            print(f\"{ds:<12} {metric:<20} ### Assistant

_source line 266_

分析完成。结果摘要：

**FewRel (80 types, 3160 pairs)**
| Metric | Spearman ρ | p-value | Sig |
|---|---|---|---|
| **SCS** | **0.218** | 2.2e-35 | *** |
| Same-parent (WordNet) | 0.097 | 5.4e-08 | *** |
| WordNet WuP | 0.073 | 4.3e-05 | *** |
| Levenshtein | 0.016 | 0.363 | ns |

**Few-NERD (63 types, 1953 pairs)**
| Metric | Spearman ρ | p-value | Sig |
|---|---|---|---|
| **SCS** | **0.166** | 1.5e-13 | *** |
| Levenshtein | 0.152 | 1.4e-11 | *** |
| Same-parent (prefix) | 0.146 | 7.8e-11 | *** |
| WordNet WuP | 0.054 | 0.070 | ns (42% pairs missing) |

核心发现：
- SCS 在两个数据集上都是最强预测指标
- FewRel 上 SCS (ρ=0.218) 远超所有 baseline，次优 same-parent 仅 0.097
- Few-NERD 上 SCS (ρ=0.166) 略优于 Levenshtein (0.152) 和 same-parent (0.146)，但差距较小，因为 Few-NERD 的层级命名结构（如 art-film, art-music）让字符串相似度和前缀匹配也有一定预测力
- WordNet WuP 在 Few-NERD 上效果差（ns），因为 42% 的复合类型名无法映射到 WordNet synset
